========================================
FILE: ./backup.js
========================================
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function getFiles(dir, fileList = []) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return fileList;
  const files = fs.readdirSync(dir);
  for (const file of files) {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, file);
    const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      if (!['node_modules', '.next', '.git', 'dist', 'build'].includes(file)) {
        fileList = getFiles(fullPath, fileList);
      }
    } else {
      // Exclude environment files, images, fonts, etc.
      if (!file.startsWith('.env') && 
          !file.endsWith('.png') && 
          !file.endsWith('.jpg') && 
          !file.endsWith('.jpeg') &&
          !file.endsWith('.gif') &&
          !file.endsWith('.ico') &&
          !file.endsWith('.svg') &&
          !file.endsWith('.ttf') &&
          !file.endsWith('.woff') &&
          !file.endsWith('.woff2') &&
          !file.endsWith('.eot')) {
        fileList.push(fullPath);
      }
    }
  }
  return fileList;
}

let files = [];
const rootItems = [
  'src', 
  'public', 
  'package.json', 
  'tailwind.config.ts', 
  'tailwind.config.js',
  'postcss.config.mjs', 
  'postcss.config.js',
  'next.config.mjs', 
  'next.config.js',
  'tsconfig.json', 
  'components.json'
];

rootItems.forEach(p => {
  const fullPath = path.join(__dirname, p);
  if (fs.existsSync(fullPath)) {
    const stat = fs.statSync(fullPath);
    if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      files = getFiles(fullPath, files);
    } else {
      files.push(fullPath);
    }
  }
});

let out = '';
files.forEach(f => {
  try {
    const relativePath = path.relative(__dirname, f);
    out += `\n\n================================================================================\n`;
    out += `FILE: ${relativePath}\n`;
    out += `================================================================================\n\n`;
    out += fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(`Error reading file ${f}: ${err.message}`);
  }
});

const backupPath = path.join(__dirname, 'site_backup.txt');
fs.writeFileSync(backupPath, out);
console.log(`Backup completed successfully. Saved to ${backupPath}`);

========================================
FILE: ./compass-v2/src/app/dashboard/page.tsx
========================================
"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { collection, query, where, getDocs } from "firebase/firestore";
import { onAuthStateChanged } from "firebase/auth";
import { db, auth } from "@/lib/firebase";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { StatTile } from "@/components/ui/StatTile";
import { OpportunityCard, KanbanColumn } from "@/components/pipeline";

const STAGES = ["Develop", "Propose", "Negotiating", "Finalise", "Pending Trade"] as const;

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const [opportunities, setOpportunities] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  // In a real app with Firebase Auth, this would use the current user's displayName
  // For the demo, we'll hardcode to the user who uploaded the data (Namra Khan)
  const sessionUser = "Namra Khan";

  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
      if (!user) {
        // Redirect to login if not authenticated
        router.push('/login');
        return;
      }

      try {
        const q = query(
          collection(db, "opportunities"),
          where("owner", "==", sessionUser) // Still using Namra Khan for the demo data
        );
        const snap = await getDocs(q);
        const opps = snap.docs.map(d => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() }));
        setOpportunities(opps);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Error loading opportunities", e);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    });

    return () => unsubscribe();
  }, [router]);

  const activeOpps = opportunities.filter(o => o.status === "active");
  const closedWonOpps = opportunities.filter(o => o.status === "won");

  const totalPipeline = activeOpps.reduce((s, o) => s + (o.amount || 0), 0);
  const weighted = activeOpps.reduce((s, o) => s + (o.weightedAmount || 0), 0);
  const closedWon = closedWonOpps.reduce((s, o) => s + (o.amount || 0), 0);

  const byStage = STAGES.reduce((acc, stage) => {
    acc[stage] = activeOpps.filter(o => o.salesStage === stage);
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, any[]>);

  const formatAud = (val: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('en-AU', { style: 'currency', currency: 'AUD', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(val);

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6 pb-24">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-light text-slate-100">My Pipeline</h1>
          <p className="text-slate-400 mt-1">Welcome back, {sessionUser}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 text-cyan-400 animate-pulse">Loading Pipeline...</div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-[#0b1020]/95 backdrop-blur-md pt-2 pb-4 -mx-4 px-4 md:mx-0 md:px-0 md:bg-transparent md:backdrop-blur-none border-b border-white/5 md:border-none shadow-sm md:shadow-none">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 md:gap-6">
              <StatTile label="Total Pipeline" value={formatAud(totalPipeline)} accent="cyan" />
              <StatTile label="Weighted Pipeline" value={formatAud(weighted)} accent="violet" />
              <StatTile label="Closed Won YTD" value={formatAud(closedWon)} accent="emerald" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col xl:grid xl:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mt-6">
            {STAGES.map(stage => (
              <KanbanColumn 
                key={stage} 
                title={stage}
                count={byStage[stage]?.length ?? 0}
                value={formatAud(byStage[stage]?.reduce((s: number, o: any) => s + (o.amount || 0), 0) ?? 0)}
              >
                {(byStage[stage] ?? []).map(o => <OpportunityCard key={o.id} opp={o} />)}
              </KanbanColumn>
            ))}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/app/importer/page.tsx
========================================
"use client";
import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { Dropzone, FileKind } from "@/components/importer/Dropzone";
import { parseCsv, mapCustomers, mapOpportunities, mapActivities, mapActuals } from "@/lib/parsers";
import { upsertCustomers, upsertOpportunities, upsertActivities, upsertActuals, logImportBatch } from "@/lib/sync";
import { getFinancialWeek } from "@/lib/financialWeek";

const MAP = {
  customers: { parse: parseCsv, map: mapCustomers, upsert: upsertCustomers },
  opportunities: { parse: parseCsv, map: mapOpportunities, upsert: upsertOpportunities },
  activity: { parse: parseCsv, map: mapActivities, upsert: upsertActivities },
  actuals: { parse: parseCsv, map: mapActuals, upsert: upsertActuals },
} as const;

export default function ImporterPage() {
  const [parsed, setParsed] = useState<Partial<Record<FileKind, any[]>>>({});
  const [isSyncing, setIsSyncing] = useState(false);
  const [syncResult, setSyncResult] = useState<string | null>(null);

  function handleParsed(kind: FileKind, text: string) {
    setParsed(p => ({ ...p, [kind]: MAP[kind].map(MAP[kind].parse(text)) }));
  }

  const opps = parsed.opportunities ?? [];
  const pipelineValue = opps.filter(o => o.status === "active").reduce((s, o) => s + o.amount, 0);
  const weightedValue = opps.filter(o => o.status === "active").reduce((s, o) => s + o.weightedAmount, 0);
  const closedWon = opps.filter(o => o.status === "won").reduce((s, o) => s + o.amount, 0);
  
  const unmatched = [...new Set([
    ...(parsed.customers ?? []).map(c => c.accountOwner),
    ...(parsed.opportunities ?? []).map(o => o.owner),
    ...(parsed.activity ?? []).map(a => a.assigned),
  ])].filter(Boolean);

  const weeklyAgg = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<string, { calls: number; meetings: number }>();
    for (const a of parsed.activity ?? []) {
      const { fyLabel, week } = getFinancialWeek(a.date);
      const key = `${fyLabel} W${week}`;
      const e = m.get(key) ?? { calls: 0, meetings: 0 };
      a.activityClass === "meeting" ? e.meetings++ : e.calls++;
      m.set(key, e);
    }
    return [...m.entries()].sort().slice(-8);
  }, [parsed.activity]);

  async function handleSync() {
    setIsSyncing(true);
    setSyncResult(null);
    try {
      const results = [];
      for (const kind of Object.keys(parsed) as FileKind[]) {
        const rows = parsed[kind] ?? [];
        if (rows.length > 0) {
          const upserted = await MAP[kind].upsert(rows);
          await logImportBatch(kind, "admin", rows.length, upserted, unmatched as string[]);
          results.push(`${kind}: ${upserted} records`);
        }
      }
      setSyncResult(`Sync Complete! ${results.join(" | ")}`);
    } catch (e: any) {
      setSyncResult(`Error: ${e.message}`);
    } finally {
      setIsSyncing(false);
    }
  }

  const formatAud = (val: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('en-AU', { style: 'currency', currency: 'AUD', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(val);

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-light text-slate-100">Data Importer</h1>
      
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <Dropzone label="Upload Customers" kind="customers" onParsed={handleParsed} />
        <Dropzone label="Upload Opportunities" kind="opportunities" onParsed={handleParsed} />
        <Dropzone label="Upload Activities" kind="activity" onParsed={handleParsed} />
        <Dropzone label="Upload Actual Spend" kind="actuals" onParsed={handleParsed} />
      </div>

      <div className="glass p-6 space-y-6">
        <h2 className="text-xl text-cyan-300 border-b border-white/10 pb-2">Import Preview</h2>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          <div className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Total Pipeline</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-mono text-slate-100">{formatAud(pipelineValue)}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-2">{opps.filter(o => o.status === "active").length} active deals</p>
          </div>
          
          <div className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Weighted Pipeline</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-mono text-violet-300">{formatAud(weightedValue)}</p>
          </div>
          
          <div className="bg-white/5 p-4 rounded-xl border border-white/10">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">Closed Won</p>
            <p className="text-2xl font-mono text-emerald-400">{formatAud(closedWon)}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {weeklyAgg.length > 0 && (
          <div className="space-y-3 mt-6">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider">Weekly Activity Aggregation Preview</h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-8 gap-2">
              {weeklyAgg.map(([key, counts]) => (
                <div key={key} className="bg-white/5 p-3 rounded-lg text-center border border-white/5">
                  <div className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">{key}</div>
                  <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                    <div className="text-cyan-400"><span className="text-[10px] uppercase block">Calls</span>{counts.calls}</div>
                    <div className="text-violet-400"><span className="text-[10px] uppercase block">Apps</span>{counts.meetings}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-white/10 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-400">
            {Object.keys(parsed).length === 0 ? "Upload CSVs above to see preview." : "Data parsed successfully. Ready to sync."}
          </div>
          
          <button 
            onClick={handleSync}
            disabled={isSyncing || Object.keys(parsed).length === 0}
            className="px-6 py-2 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-900 font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            {isSyncing ? "Syncing to Firestore..." : "Commit to Database"}
          </button>
        </div>

        {syncResult && (
          <div className={`p-4 rounded-lg mt-4 ${syncResult.includes("Error") ? "bg-red-500/20 text-red-300" : "bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300"}`}>
            {syncResult}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/app/leadership/page.tsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { collection, getDocs, query, orderBy, limit } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
import { ActivityTrendChart } from "@/components/leadership/ActivityTrendChart";
import { Leaderboard } from "@/components/leadership/Leaderboard";
import { getFinancialWeek } from "@/lib/financialWeek";

export default function LeadershipPage() {
  const [twiwData, setTwiwData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [activityTrend, setActivityTrend] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [bdmData, setBdmData] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadData() {
      try {
        // Load latest TWIW
        const twiwSnap = await getDocs(query(collection(db, "twiwSubmissions"), orderBy("submittedAt", "desc"), limit(10)));
        setTwiwData(twiwSnap.docs.map(d => d.data()));

        // Activity Trend
        const actSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, "activities"));
        const m = new Map<string, { calls: number; meetings: number }>();
        actSnap.forEach(d => {
          const a = d.data();
          if (!a.date) return;
          const { fyLabel, week } = getFinancialWeek(a.date.toDate ? a.date.toDate() : new Date(a.date.seconds * 1000));
          const key = `W${week}`;
          const e = m.get(key) ?? { calls: 0, meetings: 0 };
          a.activityClass === "meeting" ? e.meetings++ : e.calls++;
          m.set(key, e);
        });
        const trend = Array.from(m.entries())
          .map(([week, counts]) => ({ week, ...counts }))
          .sort((a, b) => parseInt(a.week.replace("W", "")) - parseInt(b.week.replace("W", "")))
          .slice(-12);
        setActivityTrend(trend);

        // Leaderboard stats
        const oppsSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, "opportunities"));
        const bdmStats = new Map<string, any>();
        
        oppsSnap.forEach(d => {
          const o = d.data();
          if (!o.owner) return;
          const bdm = bdmStats.get(o.owner) ?? { name: o.owner, ytdRevenue: 0, pipelineValue: 0, targetRevenue: 1000000 };
          
          if (o.status === "active") {
            bdm.pipelineValue += (o.amount || 0);
          } else if (o.status === "won") {
            bdm.ytdRevenue += (o.amount || 0);
          }
          bdmStats.set(o.owner, bdm);
        });

        // Also add customers YTD revenue if available
        const custSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, "customers"));
        custSnap.forEach(d => {
          const c = d.data();
          if (!c.accountOwner) return;
          const bdm = bdmStats.get(c.accountOwner) ?? { name: c.accountOwner, ytdRevenue: 0, pipelineValue: 0, targetRevenue: 1000000 };
          bdm.ytdRevenue += (c.ytdRevenue || 0);
          bdmStats.set(c.accountOwner, bdm);
        });

        setBdmData(Array.from(bdmStats.values()).sort((a, b) => b.ytdRevenue - a.ytdRevenue));

      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }
    loadData();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="p-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-light text-slate-100">GM Weekly Review</h1>
      
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-8">
          <section className="space-y-4">
            <h2 className="text-xl text-cyan-300">Latest TWIW Submissions</h2>
            {isLoading ? (
              <div className="animate-pulse h-32 bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10" />
            ) : (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                {twiwData.map((t, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="glass p-5 space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-white/10 pb-2">
                      <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-200">{t.bdmId.replace("-", " ").toUpperCase()}</h3>
                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{t.fyLabel} W{t.financialWeek}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">Major Updates</p>
                      <p className="text-sm text-slate-300 line-clamp-2">{t.majorUpdates || "No updates provided."}</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/5">
                      <div className="text-center bg-white/5 rounded p-2">
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">Calls</p>
                        <p className="text-cyan-400 font-mono">{t.kpi?.calls?.actual || 0}</p>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-center bg-white/5 rounded p-2">
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase">Meetings</p>
                        <p className="text-violet-400 font-mono">{t.kpi?.meetings?.actual || 0}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
                {twiwData.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="col-span-2 text-center py-10 text-slate-500 border border-dashed border-white/10 rounded-xl">
                    No submissions yet this week.
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section>
            {isLoading ? (
              <div className="animate-pulse h-64 bg-white/5 rounded-xl border border-white/10" />
            ) : (
              <Leaderboard bdmData={bdmData} />
            )}
          </section>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <h2 className="text-xl text-violet-300">Activity Trend</h2>
          <div className="glass p-5">
            {isLoading ? (
              <div className="animate-pulse h-64 bg-white/5 rounded-xl" />
            ) : activityTrend.length > 0 ? (
              <ActivityTrendChart data={activityTrend} />
            ) : (
              <div className="h-64 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500">
                No activity data
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/app/login/page.tsx

"use client";
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { signInWithEmailAndPassword } from 'firebase/auth';
import { auth } from '@/lib/firebase';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("bjmackenzie@gmail.com"); // Fallback to user's known testing email or empty
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleLogin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");
    try {
      await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
      router.push('/dashboard');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error(err);
      setError(err.message || "Failed to sign in");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4">
      <div className="glass p-10 max-w-md w-full space-y-8 text-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-widest text-cyan-400 mb-2">COMPASS <span className="text-violet-400">V2</span></h1>
          <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm">Sign in to your account</p>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
          {error && <div className="text-red-400 text-sm bg-red-400/10 p-3 rounded">{error}</div>}
          <input 
            type="email" 
            placeholder="Email Address" 
            className="w-full bg-black/20 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-slate-200 outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
          <input 
            type="password" 
            placeholder="Password" 
            className="w-full bg-black/20 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-slate-200 outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
            value={password}
            onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            required
          />
          
          <button 
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-violet-500 hover:from-cyan-400 hover:to-violet-400 text-white font-bold rounded-lg transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? "Signing In..." : "Sign In"}
          </button>
        </form>
        
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-8">
          Connected to Firebase Auth. Use your production credentials to test V2.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

            Documentation
          </a>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/app/twiw/page.tsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { collection, query, where, getDocs, addDoc, serverTimestamp } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
import { getFinancialWeek } from "@/lib/financialWeek";
import { StatTile } from "@/components/ui/StatTile";

export default function TwiwPage() {
  const sessionUser = "Namra Khan"; // Mock
  
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [wins, setWins] = useState([{ customerName: "", value: 0, businessUnit: "", notes: "" }]);
  const [majorUpdates, setMajorUpdates] = useState("");
  const [kpi, setKpi] = useState({
    calls: { actual: 0, target: 25 },
    meetings: { actual: 0, target: 8 },
    proposals: { actual: 0, target: 3 },
    deals: { actual: 0, target: 1 },
  });
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [statusMsg, setStatusMsg] = useState("");
  const [lastSaved, setLastSaved] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Load from local storage
  useEffect(() => {
    const draft = localStorage.getItem("twiw_draft");
    if (draft) {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(draft);
        if (parsed.wins) setWins(parsed.wins);
        if (parsed.majorUpdates) setMajorUpdates(parsed.majorUpdates);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Failed to parse draft");
      }
    }
  }, []);

  // Auto-save
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      localStorage.setItem("twiw_draft", JSON.stringify({ wins, majorUpdates }));
      setLastSaved(new Date().toLocaleTimeString());
    }, 2000);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [wins, majorUpdates]);

  // Load KPIs
  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadActuals() {
      const q = query(
        collection(db, "activities"),
        where("assigned", "==", sessionUser)
      );
      const snap = await getDocs(q);
      
      let calls = 0, meetings = 0;
      snap.forEach(d => {
        const data = d.data();
        if (data.activityClass === "meeting") meetings++;
        else calls++;
      });
      
      setKpi(k => ({ 
        ...k, 
        calls: { ...k.calls, actual: calls },
        meetings: { ...k.meetings, actual: meetings } 
      }));
    }
    loadActuals();
  }, []);

  async function submit(status: "draft" | "submitted") {
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const { fyLabel, week } = getFinancialWeek(new Date());
      await addDoc(collection(db, "twiwSubmissions"), {
        uid: "mock-uid", 
        bdmId: "namra-khan",
        financialWeek: week, 
        fyLabel, 
        submittedAt: serverTimestamp(),
        wins, 
        majorUpdates, 
        kpi, 
        status,
      });
      setStatusMsg("Successfully submitted TWIW!");
      localStorage.removeItem("twiw_draft");
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      setStatusMsg("Error submitting.");
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6 pb-24">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-light text-slate-100">Submit Weekly Review</h1>
        {lastSaved && <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">Draft saved: {lastSaved}</span>}
      </div>

      {/* Progress Bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center mb-6">
        {[1, 2, 3, 4].map(s => (
          <div key={s} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center">
            <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold ${step >= s ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-white' : 'bg-white/10 text-slate-500'}`}>
              {s}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      <div className="glass p-4 md:p-6 space-y-4">
        {step === 1 && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-right-4 duration-300">
            <h2 className="text-xl text-emerald-300 mb-4">Step 1: Major Wins</h2>
            {wins.map((w, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  placeholder="Customer Name" 
                  className="w-full flex-1 bg-black/20 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-slate-200 outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                  value={w.customerName}
                  onChange={e => {
                    const newWins = [...wins];
                    newWins[i].customerName = e.target.value;
                    setWins(newWins);
                  }}
                />
                <input 
                  type="number" 
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  placeholder="Value ($)" 
                  className="w-full md:w-32 bg-black/20 border border-white/10 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-slate-200 outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                  value={w.value || ''}
                  onChange={e => {
                    const newWins = [...wins];
                    newWins[i].value = Number(e.target.value);
                    setWins(newWins);
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
            <button 
              onClick={() => setWins([...wins, { customerName: "", value: 0, businessUnit: "", notes: "" }])}
              className="text-sm text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 py-2 w-full md:w-auto text-left"
            >
              + Add another win
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 2 && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-right-4 duration-300">
            <h2 className="text-xl text-cyan-300 mb-4">Step 2: Updates & Roadblocks</h2>
            <textarea 
              rows={6}
              className="w-full bg-black/20 border border-white/10 rounded-lg p-4 text-slate-200 outline-none focus:border-cyan-400 resize-none text-base"
              placeholder="What happened this week? Any risks or support needed?"
              value={majorUpdates}
              onChange={e => setMajorUpdates(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 3 && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-right-4 duration-300">
            <h2 className="text-xl text-violet-300 mb-4">Step 3: KPI Actuals vs Target</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <StatTile label="Calls (Actual / Target)" value={`${kpi.calls.actual} / ${kpi.calls.target}`} accent="cyan" />
              <StatTile label="Meetings (Actual / Target)" value={`${kpi.meetings.actual} / ${kpi.meetings.target}`} accent="violet" />
              <StatTile label="Proposals" value={`${kpi.proposals.actual} / ${kpi.proposals.target}`} accent="amber" />
              <StatTile label="Deals Won" value={`${kpi.deals.actual} / ${kpi.deals.target}`} accent="emerald" />
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">These are auto-calculated from your logged activities.</p>
          </div>
        )}

        {step === 4 && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-right-4 duration-300">
            <h2 className="text-xl text-amber-300 mb-4">Step 4: Final Review</h2>
            <div className="bg-black/20 rounded-lg p-4 border border-white/5 space-y-4 text-sm">
              <div>
                <p className="text-slate-500 font-semibold mb-1">Total Wins Logged</p>
                <p className="text-slate-200">{wins.filter(w => w.customerName).length} wins</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-slate-500 font-semibold mb-1">Updates</p>
                <p className="text-slate-200">{majorUpdates || "None provided"}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {statusMsg && (
              <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-lg text-center font-semibold">
                {statusMsg}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Navigation Buttons */}
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-6 mt-6 border-t border-white/10">
          <button 
            onClick={() => setStep(s => Math.max(1, s - 1))}
            disabled={step === 1 || isSubmitting}
            className="px-6 py-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-slate-300 font-semibold rounded-lg transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            Back
          </button>
          
          {step < 4 ? (
            <button 
              onClick={() => setStep(s => Math.min(4, s + 1))}
              className="px-8 py-3 bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-900 font-bold rounded-lg transition-all"
            >
              Next
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button 
              onClick={() => submit("submitted")}
              disabled={isSubmitting || !!statusMsg}
              className="px-8 py-3 bg-emerald-500 hover:bg-emerald-400 text-slate-900 font-bold rounded-lg transition-all disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Submit to GM
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/components/importer/Dropzone.tsx
========================================
"use client";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { useDropzone } from "react-dropzone";

export type FileKind = "customers" | "opportunities" | "activity" | "actuals";

export function Dropzone({ label, kind, onParsed }: {
  label: string; kind: FileKind; onParsed: (kind: FileKind, text: string) => void;
}) {
  const [file, setFile] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  const onDrop = useCallback(([f]: File[]) => {
    if (!f) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => { 
      onParsed(kind, reader.result as string); 
      setFile(f.name); 
    };
    reader.readAsText(f);
  }, [kind, onParsed]);

  const { getRootProps, getInputProps, isDragActive } = useDropzone({
    onDrop, accept: { "text/csv": [".csv"] }, maxFiles: 1,
  });

  return (
    <div {...getRootProps()} className={`
      rounded-2xl border border-dashed p-5 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer text-center
      ${isDragActive
        ? "border-cyan-300/70 bg-cyan-400/10 scale-[1.02]"
        : "border-white/15 bg-white/[0.04] hover:bg-white/[0.07]"}`}>
      <input {...getInputProps()} />
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-200">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
        {file ? <span className="text-cyan-400">✓ {file}</span> : "Drop CSV or click to browse"}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export function Leaderboard({ bdmData }: { bdmData: any[] }) {
  const formatAud = (val: number) => new Intl.NumberFormat('en-AU', { 
    style: 'currency', currency: 'AUD', maximumFractionDigits: 0 
  }).format(val || 0);

  return (
    <div className="glass p-5 space-y-4">
      <h2 className="text-xl text-emerald-300">Team Leaderboard</h2>
      
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {bdmData.map((bdm, i) => {
          const revPercent = Math.min(100, Math.round(((bdm.ytdRevenue || 0) / (bdm.targetRevenue || 1)) * 100));
          
          return (
            <div key={i} className="bg-white/5 rounded-xl p-4 border border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 justify-between items-center">
              
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 w-full md:w-1/3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-400 to-cyan-500 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold shrink-0">
                  {bdm.name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-200">{bdm.name}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">BDM</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="w-full md:w-1/3 flex flex-col gap-1">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">YTD Revenue</span>

                  />
                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="w-full md:w-1/3 flex flex-col gap-1">

                  <span className="text-slate-400">Active Pipeline</span>

                </div>
              </div>
              
            </div>
          );
        })}
        {bdmData.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-6 text-slate-500">No BDM data available</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function StatTile({ label, value, accent }: { label: string, value: string, accent: 'cyan' | 'violet' | 'emerald' | 'amber' }) {
  const borderColors = {
    cyan: 'border-cyan-400/30',
    violet: 'border-violet-400/30',
    emerald: 'border-emerald-400/30',
    amber: 'border-amber-400/30',
  };
  const textColors = {
    cyan: 'text-cyan-400',
    violet: 'text-violet-400',
    emerald: 'text-emerald-400',
    amber: 'text-amber-400',
  };

  return (
    <div className={`glass p-6 card-hover border-l-4 ${borderColors[accent]}`}>
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-400 mb-2">{label}</p>
      <h3 className={`text-3xl font-mono ${textColors[accent]}`}>{value}</h3>
    </div>
  );
}

========================================
FILE: ./compass-v2/src/lib/financialWeek.ts
========================================
export function getFyAnchor(fyStartYear: number): Date {
  // fyStartYear = calendar year April begins, e.g. 2025 for FY26
  const apr = new Date(fyStartYear, 3, 1);           // 1 April
  const day = apr.getDay();                          // 0 Sun..6 Sat
  const offset = (day === 1) ? 0 : (day === 0 ? 1 : 8 - day);
  return new Date(fyStartYear, 3, 1 + offset);       // first Monday of April
}

export function fyLabelFor(date: Date): { fyLabel: string; fyStartYear: number } {
  const y = date.getFullYear();
  const fyStartYear = date.getMonth() >= 3 ? y : y - 1;  // April = month 3
  return { fyLabel: `FY${(fyStartYear + 1) % 100}`, fyStartYear };
}

export function getFinancialWeek(date: Date): { fyLabel: string; week: number } {
  const { fyLabel, fyStartYear } = fyLabelFor(date);
  const anchor = getFyAnchor(fyStartYear);
  const ms = Math.floor(
    (startOfDay(date).getTime() - anchor.getTime()) / 86_400_000
  );
  const week = Math.floor(ms / 7) + 1;               // Week 1 = anchor week
  return { fyLabel, week };
}

function startOfDay(d: Date) {
  return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
}

export function weekRange(fyStartYear: number, week: number) {
  const anchor = getFyAnchor(fyStartYear);
  const start = new Date(anchor.getTime() + (week - 1) * 7 * 86_400_000);
  const end = new Date(start.getTime() + 7 * 86_400_000);
  return { start, end };
}

import { initializeApp, getApps, getApp } from "firebase/app";
import { initializeFirestore, persistentLocalCache, persistentMultipleTabManager } from "firebase/firestore";
import { getAuth } from "firebase/auth";

const firebaseConfig = {
  apiKey: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY,
  authDomain: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN,
  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  storageBucket: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET,
  messagingSenderId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_MESSAGING_SENDER_ID,
  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID
};

const app = !getApps().length ? initializeApp(firebaseConfig) : getApp();

const db = initializeFirestore(app, {
  localCache: persistentLocalCache({
    tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager()
  })
});

const auth = getAuth(app);

export { app, db, auth };

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/lib/parsers/index.ts

import Papa from "papaparse";
import { createHash } from "crypto";

export function parseCsv(text: string) {
  const { data } = Papa.parse<Record<string, string>>(text, {
    header: true, skipEmptyLines: true, transformHeader: h => h.trim(),
  });
  return data;
}

export const num = (v?: string) => parseFloat((v ?? "").replace(/[,$ ]/g, "")) || 0;

export const auDate = (v?: string) => {         // "15/10/2026"
  const [d, m, y] = (v ?? "").split("/").map(Number);
  return y && m && d ? new Date(y, m - 1, d) : null;
};

// --- Customers ---
export function mapCustomers(rows: Record<string, string>[]) {
  return rows.map(r => ({
    customerId: r["Customer ID"]?.trim(),
    accountOwner: r["Account Owner"]?.trim(),
    accountName: r["Account Name"]?.trim(),
    ytdRevenue: num(r["YTD Revenue This FY"]),
    creditHold: (r["Credit Hold"] ?? "").toLowerCase() === "yes",
    businessUnit: r["Business Unit"]?.trim(),
  })).filter(c => c.customerId);
}

// --- Opportunities ---
const ACTIVE = new Set(["Develop", "Propose", "Negotiating", "Finalise", "Pending Trade"]);

export function mapOpportunities(rows: Record<string, string>[]) {
  return rows.flatMap(r => {
    const stage = r["Sales Stage"]?.trim();
    if (stage === "Closed Lost") return [];                       // rule: ignored
    const amount = num(r["Amount"]);
    const prob = num(r["Probability (%)"]);
    const exp = auDate(r["Expected Trading Date"]);
    return [{
      opportunityId: r["Opportunity ID"]?.trim(),
      customerId: r["Customer ID"]?.trim(),
      owner: r["Opportunity Owner"]?.trim(),
      salesStage: stage,
      status: stage === "Closed Won" ? "won" : "active",
      amount, probability: prob, weightedAmount: amount * prob / 100,
      expectedTradingDate: exp, opportunityName: r["Opportunity Name"]?.trim(),
    }];
  }).filter(o => o.opportunityId);
}

// --- Activities ---
const MEETING_PAT = /video|conference|face to face|visit/i;

export function mapActivities(rows: Record<string, string>[]) {
  return rows.flatMap(r => {
    const status = (r["Status"] ?? "").trim();
    if (status.toLowerCase() === "not started") return [];        // rule: ignore
    const date = auDate(r["Date"]);
    const assigned = r["Assigned"]?.trim();
    const customerId = r["Customer ID"]?.trim();
    const type = r["Activity Type"]?.trim();
    if (!date || !assigned || !type || !customerId) return [];

    const dedupHash = createHash("sha256")
      .update([date.toDateString(), assigned, type, customerId].join("|"))
      .digest("hex");

    return [{
      dedupHash, date, assigned, customerId, activityType: type,
      company: r["Company / Customer"]?.trim(),
      subject: r["Subject"]?.trim(),
      activityClass: MEETING_PAT.test(type) ? "meeting" : "call",
      status, completed: (r["Completed?"] ?? "").trim() === "1",
    }];
  });
}

// --- Actuals ---
export function mapActuals(rows: Record<string, string>[]) {
  return rows.map(r => ({
    commonCustomerName: r["Common Customer Name"]?.trim(),
    businessUnit: r["Business Unit"]?.trim(),
    customerId: r["Account"]?.trim(),     // "Account" holds the SF ID
    lineOfBusiness: r["Line of Business"]?.trim(),
    amount: num(r["Key Measures[Detailed Value]"]),
    category: r["Category"]?.trim(),
  })).filter(a => a.customerId);
}

FILE: ./compass-v2/src/lib/parsers/parsers.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { num, auDate, mapCustomers, mapOpportunities, mapActivities } from './index';

describe('Parser Utilities', () => {
  it('num() parses standard numbers', () => {
    expect(num("100")).toBe(100);
    expect(num("100.5")).toBe(100.5);
  });

  it('num() handles currency and commas', () => {
    expect(num("$1,000.50")).toBe(1000.5);
    expect(num(" 1,000 ")).toBe(1000);
  });

  it('num() returns 0 for invalid inputs', () => {
    expect(num("")).toBe(0);
    expect(num(undefined)).toBe(0);
    expect(num("invalid")).toBe(0);
  });

  it('auDate() parses DD/MM/YYYY', () => {
    const d = auDate("15/10/2026");
    expect(d?.getFullYear()).toBe(2026);
    expect(d?.getMonth()).toBe(9); // 0-indexed
    expect(d?.getDate()).toBe(15);
  });

  it('auDate() returns null for invalid dates', () => {
    expect(auDate("")).toBeNull();
    expect(auDate("invalid")).toBeNull();
    expect(auDate(undefined)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('Customers Parser', () => {
  it('maps valid customer rows', () => {
    const rows = [{
      "Customer ID": "C001",
      "Account Owner": "Namra Khan",
      "Account Name": "Acme Corp",
      "YTD Revenue This FY": "$50,000",
      "Credit Hold": "Yes",
      "Business Unit": "Premium"
    }];
    const result = mapCustomers(rows);
    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result[0].customerId).toBe("C001");
    expect(result[0].ytdRevenue).toBe(50000);
    expect(result[0].creditHold).toBe(true);
  });

  it('filters out rows without Customer ID', () => {
    const rows = [{ "Account Name": "Acme Corp" }];
    expect(mapCustomers(rows)).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

describe('Opportunities Parser', () => {
  it('maps valid opportunities and computes weighted amount', () => {
    const rows = [{
      "Opportunity ID": "O001",
      "Customer ID": "C001",
      "Sales Stage": "Develop",
      "Amount": "$10,000",
      "Probability (%)": "50",
      "Expected Trading Date": "15/10/2026"
    }];
    const result = mapOpportunities(rows);
    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result[0].weightedAmount).toBe(5000); // 10000 * 50 / 100
    expect(result[0].status).toBe("active");
  });

  it('ignores Closed Lost opportunities', () => {
    const rows = [{ "Sales Stage": "Closed Lost", "Opportunity ID": "O001" }];
    expect(mapOpportunities(rows)).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('marks Closed Won as won status', () => {
    const rows = [{ "Sales Stage": "Closed Won", "Opportunity ID": "O001" }];
    expect(mapOpportunities(rows)[0].status).toBe("won");
  });
});

describe('Activities Parser', () => {
  it('maps valid activities and generates dedup hash', () => {
    const rows = [{
      "Date": "15/10/2026",
      "Assigned": "Namra Khan",
      "Activity Type": "Face to face",
      "Customer ID": "C001",
      "Status": "Completed",
      "Completed?": "1"
    }];
    const result = mapActivities(rows);
    expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result[0].activityClass).toBe("meeting");
    expect(result[0].completed).toBe(true);
    expect(typeof result[0].dedupHash).toBe("string");
    expect(result[0].dedupHash.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('classifies calls correctly', () => {
    const rows = [{
      "Date": "15/10/2026",
      "Assigned": "Namra Khan",
      "Activity Type": "Phone call",
      "Customer ID": "C001",
      "Status": "Completed"
    }];
    expect(mapActivities(rows)[0].activityClass).toBe("call");
  });

  it('ignores Not Started status', () => {
    const rows = [{ "Status": "Not Started", "Date": "15/10/2026", "Assigned": "Namra", "Activity Type": "Call", "Customer ID": "C001" }];
    expect(mapActivities(rows)).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('ignores rows with missing critical fields', () => {
    const rows = [{ "Date": "15/10/2026" }]; // Missing assigned, type, customerId
    expect(mapActivities(rows)).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});

========================================
FILE: ./compass-v2/src/lib/sync.ts
========================================
import { doc, writeBatch, serverTimestamp, collection, addDoc } from "firebase/firestore";
import { db } from "./firebase";

export async function upsertCustomers(rows: any[]) {
  let upserted = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 450) {                  // batch ≤500
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    for (const r of rows.slice(i, i + 450)) {
      batch.set(doc(db, "customers", r.customerId),
        { ...r, lastSyncedAt: serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
      upserted++;
    }
    await batch.commit();
  }
  return upserted;
}

export async function upsertOpportunities(rows: any[]) {
  let upserted = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 450) {
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    for (const r of rows.slice(i, i + 450)) {
      batch.set(doc(db, "opportunities", r.opportunityId),
        { ...r, lastSyncedAt: serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
      upserted++;
    }
    await batch.commit();
  }
  return upserted;
}

export async function upsertActivities(rows: any[]) {
  let upserted = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 450) {
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    for (const r of rows.slice(i, i + 450)) {
      batch.set(doc(db, "activities", r.dedupHash),
        { ...r, lastSyncedAt: serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true });
      upserted++;
    }
    await batch.commit();
  }
  return upserted;
}

export async function upsertActuals(rows: any[]) {
  let upserted = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += 450) {
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    for (const r of rows.slice(i, i + 450)) {
      const actualRef = doc(collection(db, "actualRevenues"));
      batch.set(actualRef, { ...r, lastSyncedAt: serverTimestamp() });
      upserted++;
    }
    await batch.commit();
  }
  return upserted;
}

export async function logImportBatch(fileKind: string, uploadedBy: string, rowsParsed: number, rowsUpserted: number, unmatchedUsers: string[]) {
  await addDoc(collection(db, "importBatches"), {
    fileKind,
    uploadedBy,
    rowsParsed,
    rowsUpserted,
    unmatchedUsers,
    createdAt: serverTimestamp()
  });
}

========================================
FILE: ./create_backup.py
========================================
import os

backup_file = "nw.txt"
root_dir = "."

ignore_dirs = {'.git', 'node_modules', '.next', 'dist', 'build', '.gemini'}
allowed_exts = {'.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.css', '.json'}

def generate_tree(dir_path, prefix=""):
    tree_str = ""
    try:
        entries = sorted(os.listdir(dir_path))
    except PermissionError:
        return ""
        
    entries = [e for e in entries if e not in ignore_dirs and not e.startswith('.')]
    
    for i, entry in enumerate(entries):
        path = os.path.join(dir_path, entry)
        is_last = i == len(entries) - 1
        connector = "└── " if is_last else "├── "
        tree_str += f"{prefix}{connector}{entry}\n"
        
        if os.path.isdir(path):
            extension = "    " if is_last else "│   "
            tree_str += generate_tree(path, prefix + extension)
            
    return tree_str

with open(backup_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write("=== SITE ARCHITECTURE ===\n\n")
    f.write(generate_tree(root_dir))
    f.write("\n\n=== SOURCE CODE ===\n\n")
    
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ignore_dirs and not d.startswith('.')]
        
        for file in files:
            if file == "nw.txt" or file.endswith(".py"):
                continue
                
            _, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
            if ext in allowed_exts:
                filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
                
                # Exclude package-lock.json and similar huge generated files
                if file in ["package-lock.json", "yarn.lock", "pnpm-lock.yaml"]:
                    continue
                    
                try:
                    with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as infile:
                        content = infile.read()
                        
                        # Sanitize API keys
                        if "apiKey" in content or "NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY" in content:
                            content = content.replace(content, "/* File content hidden due to API keys */")
                            
                        f.write(f"\n\n--- FILE: {filepath} ---\n\n")
                        f.write(content)
                except Exception as e:
                    f.write(f"\n\n--- FILE: {filepath} (Error reading file) ---\n\n")

print(f"Successfully created {backup_file}")

========================================
FILE: ./create_compassAug_backup.py
========================================
import os
import re

EXCLUDE_DIRS = {'.git', 'node_modules', '.next', 'public', 'scratch', 'scripts', 'docs'}
EXCLUDE_FILES = {
    '.env', '.env.local', '.env.development', '.env.production', '.DS_Store',
    'package-lock.json', 'compassAug.txt', 'tsconfig.tsbuildinfo'
}
EXCLUDE_EXTS = {'.txt', '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.svg', '.ico', '.pyc', '.md', '.py', '.sh'}
INCLUDE_EXTS = {'.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.json', '.css', '.mjs', '.yaml', '.html'}

def redact_keys(content):
    # redact common API keys (Firebase, OpenAI, etc)
    content = re.sub(r'AIza[0-9A-Za-z-_]{35}', '[REDACTED_API_KEY]', content)
    content = re.sub(r'(?i)(api_?key\s*[:=]\s*["\']?)[a-zA-Z0-9-_]{20,}(["\']?)', r'\1[REDACTED]\2', content)
    content = re.sub(r'sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{32,}', '[REDACTED_API_KEY]', content)
    return content

def main():
    output_file = 'compassAug.txt'
    with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as outfile:
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk('.'):
            # modify dirs in-place to skip excluded directories
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDE_DIRS and not d.startswith('.')]
            
            for file in files:
                if file in EXCLUDE_FILES or file.startswith('.env'):
                    continue
                ext = os.path.splitext(file)[1]
                if ext not in INCLUDE_EXTS:
                    continue

                filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
                try:
                    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as infile:
                        content = infile.read()
                        content = redact_keys(content)
                        outfile.write(f"--- File: {filepath} ---\n")
                        outfile.write(content)
                        outfile.write("\n\n")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Skipping {filepath} due to error: {e}")
    print("Backup completed successfully to compassAug.txt")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

FILE: ./fix_collated_references.py
========================================
import re

file_path = "src/components/dashboard/demo-dash-view.tsx"
with open(file_path, "r") as f:
    content = f.read()

# Fix handleExportPdf references
# Replace `collatedMockSubmissions` with `submissionsByState` in handleExportPdf HTML generation
content = content.replace("Object.entries(collatedMockSubmissions)", "Object.entries(submissionsByState)")

# Fix Key Standouts references
# Replace `collatedMockSubmissions` with `submissionsByState`
content = content.replace("Object.entries(collatedMockSubmissions)", "Object.entries(submissionsByState)")

# Wait, the PDF export HTML generation uses `sub.name`, `sub.wins.replace` but now `sub.wins` is an array of objects!
pdf_old = r"                            <td class=\"bold\">\$\{sub\.name\}<\/td>\n                            <td class=\"whitespace-pre-line\">\$\{sub\.wins\.replace\(\/\\n\/g, '<br>'\)\}<\/td>\n                            <td class=\"whitespace-pre-line rose\">\$\{sub\.risks\.replace\(\/\\n\/g, '<br>'\)\}<\/td>\n                            <td class=\"whitespace-pre-line\">\$\{sub\.updates\.replace\(\/\\n\/g, '<br>'\)\}<\/td>\n                            <td class=\"whitespace-pre-line blue\">\$\{sub\.projected\.replace\(\/\\n\/g, '<br>'\)\}<\/td>\n                            <td class=\"whitespace-pre-line\">\$\{sub\.priorities\.replace\(\/\\n\/g, '<br>'\)\}<\/td>"

pdf_new = """                            <td class="bold">${sub.name}</td>
                            <td class="whitespace-pre-line">${(sub.wins || []).map((w: any) => `• ${w.customer} ($${w.value})`).join('<br>')}</td>
                            <td class="whitespace-pre-line rose">${(sub.risks || []).map((r: any) => `• ${r.account} ($${r.value})<br>Mitigation: ${r.mitigation}`).join('<br><br>')}</td>
                            <td class="whitespace-pre-line">${[sub.updates, ...(sub.majorUpdates || []).map((m: any) => `• ${m.customer} - ${m.updateText}`)].filter(Boolean).join('<br><br>')}</td>
                            <td class="whitespace-pre-line blue">${(sub.projectedWins || []).map((p: any) => `• ${p.account} ($${p.value})<br>Expected: ${p.expectedDate}`).join('<br><br>')}</td>
                            <td class="whitespace-pre-line">${(sub.priorities || []).map((p: any) => `• ${p.text}`).join('<br>')}</td>"""

content = re.sub(pdf_old, pdf_new, content)

with open(file_path, "w") as f:
    f.write(content)

========================================
FILE: ./fix_duplicates.py
========================================
import re

file_path = "src/components/dashboard/demo-dash-view.tsx"
with open(file_path, "r") as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

new_lines = []
skip_count = 0
for i, line in enumerate(lines):
    if skip_count > 0:
        skip_count -= 1
        continue
    
    if "const [majorUpdates, setMajorUpdates] = useState<any[]>([]);" in line:
        continue # Remove duplicate state

========================================
FILE: ./generate_backup.js
========================================
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const rootDir = __dirname;
const outPath = path.join(rootDir, 'site_backup.txt');

const skipDirs = ['node_modules', '.git', '.next', 'dist', 'build', '.gemini'];
const allowedExts = ['.js', '.jsx', '.ts', '.tsx', '.css', '.json', '.md'];

function getStructure(dir, prefix = '') {
  let result = '';
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
  const filtered = entries.filter(e => !skipDirs.includes(e.name) && !e.name.startsWith('.'));
  
  filtered.forEach((e, i) => {
    const isLast = i === filtered.length - 1;
    result += `${prefix}${isLast ? '└── ' : '├── '}${e.name}\n`;
    if (e.isDirectory()) {
      result += getStructure(path.join(dir, e.name), prefix + (isLast ? '    ' : '│   '));
    }
  });
  return result;
}

function getAllFiles(dir, fileList = []) {
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
  entries.forEach(e => {
    if (skipDirs.includes(e.name) || e.name.startsWith('.')) return;
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, e.name);
    if (e.isDirectory()) {
      getAllFiles(fullPath, fileList);
    } else {
      if (allowedExts.includes(path.extname(e.name)) && e.name !== 'package-lock.json') {
        fileList.push(fullPath);
      }
    }
  });
  return fileList;
}

let output = '=== SITE STRUCTURE ===\n\n';
output += '.\n' + getStructure(rootDir);
output += '\n\n=== SOURCE CODE ===\n\n';

const files = getAllFiles(rootDir);
files.forEach(f => {
  const relPath = path.relative(rootDir, f);
  let content = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
  
  // Basic masking of API keys
  content = content.replace(/(apiKey|api_key|secret|token)["\']?\s*[:=]\s*["\'][^"\']+["\']/gi, '$1: "MASKED_API_KEY"');
  
  output += `\n\n--- FILE: ${relPath} ---\n\n`;
  output += content;
});

fs.writeFileSync(outPath, output);
console.log('Backup generated at: ' + outPath);

========================================
FILE: ./generate_backup.sh
========================================
#!/bin/bash
OUTPUT="Backup.txt"

echo "=== SITE ARCHITECTURE ===" > "$OUTPUT"
find src public package.json tsconfig.json tailwind.config.ts next.config.mjs firebase.json firestore.rules -type f 2>/dev/null | sort >> "$OUTPUT"
echo -e "\n=== SOURCE CODE ===\n" >> "$OUTPUT"

find src public -type f \( -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.tsx" -o -name "*.css" -o -name "*.js" -o -name "*.jsx" -o -name "*.json" \) | sort | while read -r file; do
    echo "---------------------------------------------------" >> "$OUTPUT"
    echo "FILE: $file" >> "$OUTPUT"
    echo "---------------------------------------------------" >> "$OUTPUT"
    cat "$file" >> "$OUTPUT"
    echo -e "\n" >> "$OUTPUT"
done

for file in package.json tsconfig.json tailwind.config.ts postcss.config.mjs next.config.mjs firebase.json firestore.rules; do
    if [ -f "$file" ]; then
        echo "---------------------------------------------------" >> "$OUTPUT"
        echo "FILE: $file" >> "$OUTPUT"
        echo "---------------------------------------------------" >> "$OUTPUT"
        cat "$file" >> "$OUTPUT"
        echo -e "\n" >> "$OUTPUT"
    fi
done

echo "Backup generated at $OUTPUT"

import type { NextConfig } from 'next';

const securityHeaders = [
  // Prevent clickjacking
  { key: 'X-Frame-Options', value: 'SAMEORIGIN' },
  // Prevent MIME type sniffing
  { key: 'X-Content-Type-Options', value: 'nosniff' },
  // Limit referrer information sent to third parties
  { key: 'Referrer-Policy', value: 'strict-origin-when-cross-origin' },
  // Disable access to sensitive browser APIs
  { key: 'Permissions-Policy', value: 'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(self), payment=()' },
  // Force HTTPS for all future requests (1 year)
  { key: 'Strict-Transport-Security', value: 'max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains' },
];

const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
  async headers() {
    return [
      {
        source: '/(.*)',
        headers: securityHeaders,
      },
    ];
  },
  images: {
    remotePatterns: [
      {
        protocol: 'https',
        hostname: 'placehold.co',
        port: '',
        pathname: '/**',
      },
      {
        protocol: 'https',
        hostname: 'images.unsplash.com',
        port: '',
        pathname: '/**',
      },
      {
        protocol: 'https',
        hostname: 'picsum.photos',
        port: '',
        pathname: '/**',
      },
    ],
  },
  experimental: {
    serverActions: {
      bodySizeLimit: '10mb',
    },
  },
};

export default nextConfig;

FILE: ./query_planning.js

import { initializeApp } from "firebase/app";
import { getFirestore, collection, getDocs, query, where } from "firebase/firestore";
import fs from 'fs';

const firebaseConfig = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('./src/firebase/config.ts', 'utf8').match(/\{[\s\S]*?\}/)[0].replace(/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+):/g, '"$1":').replace(/'/g, '"'));

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
const db = getFirestore(app);

async function run() {
  const q = query(collection(db, "weeklyCommitments"), where("week", "in", ["2026-21", "2026-22"]));
  const snap = await getDocs(q);
  const results = snap.docs.map(d => ({id: d.id, ...d.data()}));
  console.log(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));
  process.exit(0);
}
run().catch(console.error);

import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';

  projectId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID,
  storageBucket: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_STORAGE_BUCKET,

  appId: process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID,
};

  });

                    (sub.wins || []).forEach(w => {
                      if (mockItemFlags[`${sub.id}-wins-${w.id}`]?.isStarred) standouts.push({ type: 'Win', ...w });
                    });
                    (sub.risks || []).forEach(r => {
                      if (mockItemFlags[`${sub.id}-risks-${r.id}`]?.isStarred) standouts.push({ type: 'Risk', ...r });
                    });
                    (sub.majorUpdates || []).forEach(m => {
                      if (mockItemFlags[`${sub.id}-majorUpdates-${m.id}`]?.isStarred) standouts.push({ type: 'Update', ...m });
                    });
                    (sub.projectedWins || []).forEach(p => {
                      if (mockItemFlags[`${sub.id}-projectedWins-${p.id}`]?.isStarred) standouts.push({ type: 'Projected', ...p });
                    });
                    (sub.priorities || []).forEach(p => {
                      if (mockItemFlags[`${sub.id}-priorities-${p.id}`]?.isStarred) standouts.push({ type: 'Priority', ...p });
                    });

                    return standouts.map((item, idx) => (
                      <div key={`${sub.id}-${idx}`} className="bg-white border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-sm relative group flex flex-col justify-between">
                        <div className="space-y-3">
                          <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">
                            <Badge className={
                              item.type === 'Win' ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 uppercase font-black text-[9px]" :
                              item.type === 'Risk' ? "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 uppercase font-black text-[9px]" :
                              item.type === 'Projected' ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 uppercase font-black text-[9px]" :
                              item.type === 'Priority' ? "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 uppercase font-black text-[9px]" :
                              "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 uppercase font-black text-[9px]"
                            }>
                              {item.type}
                            </Badge>
                            <Star className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 fill-current" />
                          </div>
                          
                          <div>
                            <div className="font-bold text-slate-800 line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
                              {item.customer || item.account || item.text}
                            </div>
                            {item.value ? (
                              <div className={
                                item.type === 'Risk' ? "text-rose-600 font-black mt-1" : 
                                item.type === 'Win' ? "text-emerald-600 font-black mt-1" :
                                "text-blue-600 font-black mt-1"
                              }>
                                {formatEAV(item.value)}
                              </div>
                            ) : null}
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 mt-2 uppercase tracking-wider">{sub.name}</div>

                            {item.mitigation && <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-2 line-clamp-3"><span className="font-bold text-slate-700">Mitigation:</span> {item.mitigation}</div>}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                  })
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </TabsContent>
        )}"""

mock_old = r"  \/\/ Mock collation dataset \(combining user inputs with 2 other mock BDMs\)\n  const collatedMockSubmissions = useMemo\(\(\) => \{.*?      \};\n    \}\n\n    return \{\n.*?\n    \};\n  \}, \[wins, risks, projectedWins, priorities, updates, majorUpdates, currentWeekFocusAccounts, twtwRoadblocks, twtwKpiActuals\]\);"

      }

  const submissionsByState = useMemo(() => {
    return mappedSubmissions.reduce((acc, sub) => {
      if (!acc[sub.state]) acc[sub.state] = [];
      acc[sub.state].push(sub);

    }, {} as Record<string, any[]>);
  }, [mappedSubmissions]);"""

content = re.sub(mock_old, mock_new, content, flags=re.DOTALL)


# 2. Add helper functions
helpers_old = r"  const toggleMockItemFlag = \(id: string, flag: 'isStarred' \| 'isHidden'\) => \{\n    setMockItemFlags\(prev => \(\{\n      \.\.\.prev,\n      \[id\]: \{\n        \.\.\.\(prev\[id\] \|\| \{\}\),\n        \[flag\]: !prev\[id\]\?\.\[flag\]\n      \}\n    \}\)\);\n  \};"

helpers_new = """  const toggleMockItemFlag = (id: string, flag: 'isStarred' | 'isHidden') => {
    setMockItemFlags(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: {
        ...(prev[id] || {}),
        [flag]: !prev[id]?.[flag]
      }
    }));
  };

  const toggleItemState = (subId: string, type: 'wins' | 'risks' | 'majorUpdates' | 'projectedWins' | 'priorities', itemId: string, flag: 'isStarred' | 'isHidden') => {
    const key = `${subId}-${type}-${itemId}`;
    toggleMockItemFlag(key, flag);
  };

  const handleEditSubmission = (sub: any) => {
    toast({ title: 'Simulator Mode', description: 'Editing is disabled in the simulator view.' });
  };

  const handleDeleteSubmission = (id: string) => {
    toast({ title: 'Simulator Mode', description: 'Deleting is disabled in the simulator view.' });
  };

  const formatEAV = (val: number) => {
    if (val >= 1000000) return `$${(val / 1000000).toFixed(1)}M`;
    if (val >= 1000) return `$${(val / 1000).toFixed(0)}k`;
    return `$${val}`;
  };

  const renderItem = (item: any, type: string, subId: string, content: React.ReactNode) => {
    const key = `${subId}-${type}-${item.id}`;
    const flags = mockItemFlags[key] || {};
    if (flags.isHidden) return null;
    return (
      <div key={key} className="relative group p-2 mb-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100 rounded-lg hover:border-slate-200 transition-all">
        <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex flex-col gap-1 z-10">
          <Button size="icon" variant="secondary" className={`w-6 h-6 shadow-sm border bg-white ${flags.isStarred ? 'border-amber-400 text-amber-500' : 'border-slate-200 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-500'}`} onClick={() => toggleItemState(subId, type as any, item.id, 'isStarred')}>
            <Star className={`w-3 h-3 ${flags.isStarred ? 'fill-current' : ''}`} />
          </Button>
          <Button size="icon" variant="secondary" className="w-6 h-6 shadow-sm border border-slate-200 bg-white hover:text-slate-600 text-slate-400" onClick={() => toggleItemState(subId, type as any, item.id, 'isHidden')}>
            <EyeOff className="w-3 h-3" />
          </Button>
        </div>
        <div className="pr-8">
          {content}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };"""

content = re.sub(helpers_old, helpers_new, content, flags=re.DOTALL)

# 3. Replace TabsContent collation
tabs_old = r"        \{\/\* --- TAB 3: EXECUTIVE COLLATION TAB --- \*\/}.*?        \{\/\* ==========================================\n         \* KEY STANDOUTS VIEW"

        {isLeader && (
        <TabsContent value="collation" className="mt-0">
          <Card className="border shadow-md bg-white">
            <CardHeader className="bg-slate-50/50 border-b flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between py-5 gap-4">
              <div>

                <CardDescription className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest mt-0.5">

                   <div className="text-center py-24 text-slate-400 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
                     No submissions available to collate yet.
                   </div>
                ) : (
                  Object.entries(submissionsByState).map(([state, subs]) => (
                    <div key={state} className="space-y-4">
                      <h3 className="text-lg font-black uppercase text-slate-800 border-b border-slate-200 pb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                        {state} Region <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-2 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 font-black">{subs.length} Reps</Badge>

                            </tr>

                          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 bg-white">
                            {subs.map((sub, idx) => (
                              <tr key={idx} className="align-top relative group">
                                <td className="p-3 text-slate-600">
                                  {(sub.wins || []).map((w: any) => renderItem(w, 'wins', sub.id, (
                                    <>
                                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{w.customer}</div>
                                      <div className="text-emerald-600 font-semibold">{formatEAV(w.value)}</div>
                                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">{w.salespersonName || 'N/A'}</div>
                                      {w.businessUnits && w.businessUnits.length > 0 && <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 mt-1">BU: {w.businessUnits.join(', ')}</div>}
                                      {w.updateText && <div className="mt-1">{w.updateText}</div>}
                                    </>
                                  )))}
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-slate-600">
                                  {(sub.risks || []).map((r: any) => renderItem(r, 'risks', sub.id, (
                                    <>
                                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{r.account}</div>
                                      <div className="text-rose-600 font-semibold">{formatEAV(r.value)}</div>
                                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">{r.salespersonName || 'N/A'}</div>
                                      <div className="mt-1 text-slate-500">Mitigation: {r.mitigation}</div>
                                    </>
                                  )))}
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-slate-600">
                                  {sub.updates && (
                                    <div className="p-2 mb-2 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 rounded-lg whitespace-pre-wrap">{sub.updates}</div>
                                  )}
                                  {(sub.majorUpdates || []).map((m: any) => renderItem(m, 'majorUpdates', sub.id, (
                                    <>
                                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{m.customer}</div>
                                      {m.value > 0 && <div className="text-blue-600 font-semibold">{formatEAV(m.value)}</div>}
                                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">{m.salespersonName || 'N/A'}</div>
                                      {m.businessUnits && m.businessUnits.length > 0 && <div className="text-[9px] text-slate-400 mt-1">BU: {m.businessUnits.join(', ')}</div>}
                                      {m.updateText && <div className="mt-1">{m.updateText}</div>}
                                    </>
                                  )))}
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-slate-600">
                                  {(sub.projectedWins || []).map((p: any) => renderItem(p, 'projectedWins', sub.id, (
                                    <>
                                      <div className="font-bold text-slate-800">{p.account}</div>
                                      <div className="text-blue-600 font-semibold">{formatEAV(p.value)}</div>
                                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">{p.salespersonName || 'N/A'}</div>
                                      <div className="mt-1 text-slate-500">Expected: {p.expectedDate}</div>
                                      {p.updateText && <div className="mt-1 text-[10px]">{p.updateText}</div>}
                                    </>
                                  )))}
                                </td>
                                <td className="p-3 text-slate-600">
                                  {(sub.priorities || []).map((p: any) => renderItem(p, 'priorities', sub.id, (
                                    <>
                                      <div>{p.text}</div>
                                      <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1">{p.salespersonName || 'N/A'}</div>
                                    </>

                                    <Button size="sm" variant="destructive" className="w-full text-[10px] uppercase font-black" onClick={() => handleDeleteSubmission(sub.id)}><Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" /> Delete</Button>
                                  </div>
                                </td>
                              </tr>
                            ))}
                          </tbody>
                        </table>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>
        )}

        {/* ==========================================
         * KEY STANDOUTS VIEW"""

content = re.sub(tabs_old, tabs_new, content, flags=re.DOTALL)

with open(file_path, "w") as f:
    f.write(content)


========================================
FILE: ./scripts/clear-demo-data.js

const { initializeApp } = require('firebase-admin/app');
const { getFirestore } = require('firebase-admin/firestore');

try {
  initializeApp({ projectId: 'studio-5306701288-d19b1' });
} catch (error) {
  if (!/already exists/.test(error.message)) {
    console.error('Firebase initialization error', error.stack);
  }
}

const db = getFirestore();

async function deleteCollection(collectionPath, batchSize) {
  const collectionRef = db.collection(collectionPath);
  const query = collectionRef.orderBy('__name__').limit(batchSize);

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    deleteQueryBatch(db, query, resolve).catch(reject);
  });
}

async function deleteQueryBatch(db, query, resolve) {
  const snapshot = await query.get();

  const batchSize = snapshot.size;
  if (batchSize === 0) {
    resolve();
    return;
  }

  const batch = db.batch();
  snapshot.docs.forEach((doc) => {
    batch.delete(doc.ref);
  });
  await batch.commit();

  process.nextTick(() => {
    deleteQueryBatch(db, query, resolve);
  });
}

async function run() {
  console.log('Clearing twiwSubmissions...');
  await deleteCollection('twiwSubmissions', 500);
  console.log('Clearing weeklyCommitments...');
  await deleteCollection('weeklyCommitments', 500);
  console.log('Clearing weeklyProgress...');
  await deleteCollection('weeklyProgress', 500);
  console.log('Demo data cleared successfully.');
}

run().catch(console.error);

========================================
FILE: ./scripts/make-admin.js
========================================
const { initializeApp } = require('firebase-admin/app');
const { getFirestore } = require('firebase-admin/firestore');
const { getAuth } = require('firebase-admin/auth');

async function run() {
  try {
    initializeApp({ projectId: 'studio-5306701288-d19b1' });
    const db = getFirestore();
    const auth = getAuth();
    
    // Get all users from Auth
    const listUsersResult = await auth.listUsers();
    for (const userRecord of listUsersResult.users) {
      console.log(`Ensuring ${userRecord.email} (${userRecord.uid}) is LEADER...`);
      await db.collection('users').doc(userRecord.uid).set({
        uid: userRecord.uid,
        email: userRecord.email,
        name: userRecord.displayName || userRecord.email.split('@')[0],
        role: 'LEADER'
      }, { merge: true });
    }
    console.log("Done! All users are now Leaders. Refresh the page to see the Admin menu.");
  } catch(e) {
    console.error("Failed:", e);
  }
}
run();

        existing_imports = match.group(1)
        new_imports = existing_imports + ", Phone, Users, DollarSign, FileText, Target, Shield"
        content = content.replace(match.group(0), f"import {{\n  {new_imports}\n}} from 'lucide-react';")

  callsTarget: number;

  appointmentsActual: number;
  proposalsActual: number;
  dealsActual: number;
  revenueActual: number;

interface DemoDashViewProps {"""

  function getPreviousWeekKey(weekKey: string): string {
    const [yearStr, weekStr] = weekKey.split('-');
    const year = parseInt(yearStr, 10);
    const weekNum = parseInt(weekStr, 10);
    if (weekNum > 1) {
      return `${year}-${String(weekNum - 1).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  }

                ))}
